We introduce a dual-framing protocol with minimally varied prompts that use either support- or elimination-oriented framing while keeping the evaluation target fixed. To probe the internal computation, we append an untrained special token, [STATE], and treat its residual-stream activation as an intervention interface. Across both models, the two framings induce separable [STATE] activations concentrated in intermediate layers. Swapping these activations between paired prompts systematically changes predictions and improves cross-framing agreement, providing intervention-based evidence that the activations are behaviorally relevant. Beyond instance-level substitution, mean-difference steering directions derived from the dual-framing contrast exhibit more bounded layer-wise responses than matched contrastive activation addition directions under the evaluated protocol.

</details>


### [204] [Beyond Magnitude: Contrastive Routing for Modular Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01100)
*Nikolaos Xiros,Dimitrios Damianos,Maria-Eleni Zoumpoulidi,Leon Voukoutis,Vassilis Katsouros,Georgios Paraskevopoulos*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出对比路由机制（CoRM），通过将每个token的表示与层隐藏状态的指数移动平均（EMA）进行对比，而非基于绝对幅度进行路由，从而将路由信号集中到一个低维、高度可分的子空间。CoRM根据每个专家对输入token的亲和力与其对共享参考状态亲和力之间的差值来打分，使专家路由边界比Top-k基线更好地对齐语言结构。实验在九个零样本推理基准上，较标准Top-k MoE基线在Top-1和Top-2精度上分别提升+0.67至+1.69点和+1.38至+1.77点，仅增加2.9%参数和2.6%每token FLOPs。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE架构中，路由机制基于所有token共享的结构化表示进行决策，这限制了专家特化（expert specialization）的效果。作者希望通过改进路由信号的表示方式，提升路由质量与专家分工效率。

Method: 提出对比路由机制（CoRM）：将每个token与层隐藏状态的指数移动平均（EMA）做对比而非使用绝对幅度进行路由，从而将路由信号浓缩至低维高可分子空间。具体地，CoRM通过每个专家在输入token上的亲和力与其对该共享参考状态的亲和力之差（经由每专家独立的投影解释）来为专家打分。

Result: 在九个零样本推理基准上，CoRM相比标准Top-k MoE基线，Top-1精度提升+0.67至+1.69个点，Top-2精度提升+1.38至+1.77个点；额外参数成本仅2.9%，每token额外FLOPs仅2.6%。结果表明CoRM的专家路由边界比Top-k基线更显著地对齐语言结构。

Conclusion: 对比路由机制能够有效分离共享结构与token特有信息，提升MoE的路由语义对齐度和专家特化效果，同时保持极低的计算与参数开销，是一种实用且高效的MoE路由改进方案。

Abstract: In current Mixture-of-Experts architectures, routing is performed based on representations dominated by structure shared across all tokens, limiting expert specialization. We show that contrasting each token against an Exponential Moving Average of the layer's hidden states, rather than routing on absolute magnitude, concentrates the routing signal onto a low-dimensional, highly separable subspace. Building on this, we propose the Contrastive Routing Mechanism (CoRM), which scores each expert by the gap between its affinity for the incoming token and its affinity for this shared reference state, interpreted through a distinct per-expert projection. The resulting experts have routing boundaries that align with linguistic structure significantly more than the Top-k baseline. Our experiments show that CoRM improves average zero-shot accuracy by +0.67 to +1.69 points (Top-1) and +1.38 to +1.77 points (Top-2) over standard Top-k MoE baselines on nine zero-shot reasoning benchmarks, at the minimal cost of 2.9% added parameters and 2.6% added FLOPs per token.

</details>


### [205] [When Modality Gap Reduction Fails: Prediction-Level Hubness in CLIP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01103)
*Shota Sato,Hajime Kiyama,Tosho Hirasawa,Mamoru Komachi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了CLIP模型中模态差距（image-text modality gap）减小与零样本分类准确率提升之间并不总是一致的矛盾现象，从决策结构视角提出了"预测级集束性"（prediction-level hubness）这一失效模式，指出模态差距纠正可能改变类别间的相对决策结构，导致预测集中在少数类别上，从而解释了为何模态差距减小无法稳定提升CLIP零样本性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究普遍认为缩小CLIP中图像与文本表示之间的模态差距可以改善跨模态对齐和下游性能，但经验表明更小的平均图像-文本差距并不总能带来一致的准确率提升，这一矛盾现象缺乏系统性解释。

Method: 从零样本分类的决策结构出发，即选择与输入图像最相似的类别文本原型。以线性纠正（Linear correction）作为可解析处理的案例，证明模态差距纠正会改变类别间的相对决策结构并导致预测集中在少数类别子集上（称为预测级集束性）。通过多个数据集上的实验，对比线性纠正和学习式纠正方法，验证准确率下降与预测集中度增加之间的关联。

Result: 实验表明，在多个数据集上，模态差距纠正下的准确率下降始终与预测集中度增加相关联，适用于线性纠正和学习式纠正方法；预测级集束性是模态差距缩小不能一致提升零样本准确率的系统性原因。

Conclusion: 模态差距纠正的评估不应只看平均对齐程度，还应关注其对下游预测结构的影响。这一发现为理解CLIP零样本分类中模态差距纠正效果的局限性提供了决策结构层面的解释。

Abstract: Reducing the modality gap between image and text representations in CLIP is widely expected to improve cross-modal alignment and downstream performance. However, a smaller average image-text gap does not necessarily lead to consistent accuracy gains. We analyze this mismatch from the perspective of the decision structure in zero-shot classification, i.e. selecting the most similar class-text prototype for an input image. Zero-shot accuracy depends not only on average image--text alignment, but also on class-wise decision margins. Using Linear correction as an analytically tractable case, we show that modality gap correction can alter the relative decision structure among classes and cause predictions to concentrate on a small subset of classes. We refer to this output-space failure mode as prediction-level hubness. Furthermore, experiments across multiple datasets show that accuracy degradation under gap correction is consistently associated with increased prediction concentration, both for Linear correction and for learning-based correction methods. This provides a systematic explanation of why modality gap reduction does not consistently improve CLIP zero-shot accuracy from the perspective of downstream decision structure. Our results suggest that gap correction should be evaluated not only by average alignment, but also by its impact on downstream prediction structure.

</details>


### [206] [ClinTraceBench: Source-Verifiable Longitudinal Clinical Reasoning over EHR-Derived Dialogues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01111)
*Huimin Wang,Zhengyi Zhao,Yutian Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 ClinTraceBench，一个包含385个来自MIMIC-IV的带事件ID溯源验证对话的基准，用于衡量临床LLM助手在多年随访轨迹上的推理能力。通过评估八种历史表征策略（无上下文、仅末次就诊、全上下文、稠密检索、两种压缩方案、两种智能体记忆系统）在四个大模型后端上的约20万次预测，发现压缩策略会造成关系信息丢失、需要对多就诊趋势进行聚合补偿、上下文长度与弃权率呈非单调关系。在Pareto前沿上，Haiku在全上下文下优于Sonnet，颠覆了"越大越好"的直觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床LLM助手需要在多次就诊的患者轨迹上进行推理，但用于扩展规模的紧凑历史表征方法（检索、结构化时间线、LLM摘要、智能体记忆）是否保留临床推理所需的纵向信号，此前从未被系统度量过。

Method: 构建ClinTraceBench基准：包含385个由MIMIC-IV派生的带事件ID溯源对话，采用九任务分类法（T1-T9），并提供L0-L4确定性验证及L5人工审计（98.92%一致率）。对八种历史表征策略在四个后端（DeepSeek-V3, GPT-4o-mini, Haiku 4.5, Sonnet 4.6）上进行了6,271个问题的评估，共覆盖32个实验单元、200,672次预测。特别设计了T3受控注入探针来隔离压缩导致的关系损失。

Result: 四个核心发现：(SP4) 受控T3注入探针显示，即使归属句在构建前出现，Mem0、A-Mem和llm-summary仅能恢复0-5.3%的注入阳性，表明压缩会系统性丢失关系信息；(SP1) 压缩策略在多就诊趋势和跨患者比较上需支付聚合代价；(SP2) 无上下文到全上下文的差距为+29.8pp（GPT-4o-mini）到+62.7pp（Haiku）；(SP3) 弃权率与上下文长度呈非单调关系。Pareto前沿分析显示Haiku在全上下文下优于Sonnet（$25.76 vs. $106.21），颠覆了"最大后端最优"的经验法则。

Conclusion: 现有紧凑历史表征策略（检索、摘要、智能体记忆）会严重丢失临床纵向推理所需的关系信息，压缩不可靠。同时，模型选择上不应简单迷信"最大即最优"，需要根据成本-性能Pareto前沿做出权衡。

Abstract: Clinical LLM assistants must reason over multi-visit patient trajectories, yet whether the compact history representations used to scale them---retrieval, structured timelines, LLM summaries, agentic memory---preserve the longitudinal signal clinical reasoning needs has not been measured. We introduce ClinTraceBench: 385 MIMIC-IV-derived verified dialogues with event-ID provenance, a nine-task taxonomy (T1--T9), and L0--L4 deterministic + L5 human-audit validation (98.92\% agreement). We evaluate eight history representation strategies---a no-context floor, \textit{last-visit-only}, \textit{full-context}, BGE-M3 \textit{dense-retrieval}, two compression schemes, and two agentic-memory systems (\textit{Mem0}, \textit{A-Mem})---across four backbones (DeepSeek-V3, GPT-4o-mini, Haiku~4.5, Sonnet~4.6) on 6{,}271 questions: 32 cells, 200{,}672 predictions. Four findings: (SP4) a controlled T3 injection probe isolates compression-induced \textit{relation} loss---with the attribution sentence present \textit{before} construction, \textit{Mem0}, \textit{A-Mem} and \textit{llm-summary} still recover only 0--5.3\% of the injected positives; (SP1) compressed strategies pay an aggregation tax on multi-visit trends and cross-patient comparisons; (SP2) the blind-to-full gap spans $+29.8$~pp (GPT-4o-mini) to $+62.7$~pp (Haiku); (SP3) abstention scales non-monotonically with context length. On the Pareto frontier Haiku dominates Sonnet under \textit{full-context} (\$25.76 vs.\ \$106.21), inverting the ``biggest backbone wins'' heuristic.

</details>


### [207] [EDRAC: Benchmarking Arabic Dialect Reading Comprehension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01113)
*Noor Abo Mokh,Kirill Chirkunov,Teresa Lynn,Nizar Habash,Reham Marzouk,Malik H. Altakrori,Younes Samih,Muhammed Abu Odeh,Nour Rabih,Rahaf Alshahrani,Hamad Alshehhi,Hamdan Al-Ali,Muhra Almahri,Besher Hassan,Mohamed Anwar,Abed Alhakim Freihat,Preslav Nakov,Alham Fikri Aji*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了EDRAC，这是首个大规模方言阿拉伯语机器阅读理解与生成式问答基准，涵盖五种主要方言（埃及、摩洛哥、阿联酋、叙利亚和沙特阿拉伯语），包含499个来自自然口语的篇章和4,977个通过人机协同流程生成的问答对。基准测试揭示了当前评估指标在方言阿拉伯语语义质量与方言保真度上的显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 方言阿拉伯语相比现代标准阿拉伯语资源严重不足，尤其在机器阅读理解和问答领域。现有阿拉伯语QA基准主要集中于正式书面MSA或多项选择形式，很少覆盖自然口语方言，需要通过大规模基准来弥合这一差距。

Method: 构建了覆盖五种方言的EDRAC基准，包含499个源自自然口语互动的篇章和4,977个问答对。问答对通过人类与LLM协作管道生成，结合了迭代生成、LLM作为评审以及人工验证三个环节。评估采用了词法指标和语义指标对阿拉伯语中心和多语言LLM进行基准测试。

Result: 实验结果显示语义答案质量与方言保真度之间存在显著差距，暴露了现有评估指标在评估方言阿拉伯语生成任务方面的局限性。EDRAC作为一个现实且具有挑战性的基准，揭示了当前模型在此任务上的不足。

Conclusion: EDRAC为方言阿拉伯语NLP的未来研究提供了真实且具挑战性的MRC基准，填补了方言阿拉伯语阅读理解和生成式问答领域的资源空白，有望推动该方向的研究进展。

Abstract: Dialectal Arabic (DA) remains under-resourced compared to Modern Standard Arabic (MSA), particularly for machine reading comprehension (MRC) and question answering (QA). Existing Arabic QA benchmarks primarily focus on formal written MSA or multiple-choice QA, with limited coverage of naturally spoken dialects. Here, we aim to bridge this gap. We introduce EDRAC, the first large-scale benchmark for dialectal Arabic machine reading comprehension (MRC) and generative QA, covering five major dialects: Egyptian, Moroccan, Emirati, Syrian, and Saudi Arabic. EDRAC contains 499 passages derived from naturally occurring spoken interactions and 4,977 corresponding QA pairs generated through a human--LLM collaborative pipeline combining iterative generation, LLM-as-a-judge evaluation, and human verification. We benchmark Arabic-centric and multilingual LLMs on EDRAC using lexical and semantic metrics. Our results reveal substantial gaps between semantic answer quality and dialectal fidelity, highlighting the limitations of existing evaluation metrics for dialectal Arabic generation. EDRAC provides a realistic and challenging MRC benchmark for future research on dialectal Arabic NLP.

</details>


### [208] [Overfitting Mitigation via Singular Value Decomposition in Minimum Bayes Risk Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01135)
*Riza Setiawan Soetedjo,Yusuke Sakai,Hidetaka Kamigaito,Katsuhiko Hayashi,Taro Watanabe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SVD-MBR方法，通过对最小贝叶斯风险解码中的成对效用矩阵进行奇异值分解低秩近似，来去除度量噪声、缓解度量过拟合问题，实验显示在多种泛化度量上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最小贝叶斯风险（MBR）解码虽然能通过效用度量选择高质量假设，但极易发生度量过拟合：会不规则地抬高所选度量指标，同时损害其他未优化的评估指标，因此需要一种正则化方法来缓解该问题。

Method: 将成对效用矩阵视为含噪信息信号，通过奇异值分解（SVD）计算低秩近似并仅保留前k个主成分，从而将真实共识与度量噪声解耦，实现解码过程的去噪与正则化。

Result: 实验表明SVD-MBR能有效正则化解码过程，在一系列泛化度量上取得显著增益；同时发现该去噪效果与度量类型相关：神经度量编码了稳健的低秩共识信号，适合SVD处理，而表面级度量难以将信号与度量噪声分离。

Conclusion: SVD-MBR通过低秩矩阵近似成功缓解了MBR解码中的度量过拟合问题，但去噪有效性依赖于底层度量的信号质量，神经度量比表面级度量更适合该框架。

Abstract: Minimum Bayes Risk (MBR) decoding enables high-quality text generation by selecting the hypothesis that maximizes a utility metric over sampled pseudo-references. However, it is highly susceptible to metric overfitting: it can irregularly inflate the chosen utility metric at the direct expense of other unoptimized evaluation metrics. To mitigate this, we introduce SVD-MBR, which frames the pairwise utility matrix as a noisy information signal. By computing a low-rank approximation via Singular Value Decomposition (SVD) and retaining only the top-$k$ components, we effectively decouple true consensus from metric noise. Experiments demonstrate that SVD-MBR successfully regularizes decoding, yielding substantial gains across a range of generalized metrics. Furthermore, we reveal that this denoising is metric-dependent: neural metrics encode a robust low-rank consensus ideal for SVD, whereas surface-level metrics struggle to separate signal from metric noise.

</details>


### [209] [Does task decomposition improve automatic NLG evaluation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01139)
*Sebastian Steindl,Nikos Voskarides,Alberto Gasparin,Diego Marcheggiani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了LLM-as-a-judge框架中有无任务分解的评估方法，发现任务分解本身并不能带来性能提升，先前报道的增益实际来源于使用人类标签作为训练数据而非分解本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-judge框架被广泛用于NLG评估，先前的技术改进将评估任务分解为更简单的子任务以提升表现，但缺乏系统性的公平比较，现有增益来源不明。

Method: 在多个NLG数据集上系统地对比了带任务分解与不带任务分解的LLMaJ方法，通过设立公平基线（对照实验）来隔离任务分解与人类标签训练数据带来的影响。

Result: 发现任务分解相对于公平基线（不采用分解）并未带来性能增益；此前分解式LLMaJ报道的性能提升实际源于训练阶段使用了人类标签；当人类标签可用时，不使用任务分解的LLMaJ可以达到接近人类标注者的水平。

Conclusion: 任务分解本身不是LLMaJ性能提升的根源，人类标签训练数据才是关键因素；这提醒研究者需在公平对照下评估方法贡献，避免将训练数据效应误归因于方法设计。

Abstract: The LLM-as-a-judge (LLMaJ) framework has emerged as a promising solution for cheap, reproducible, reference-free Natural Language Generation (NLG) evaluation. Prior work seeks to improve LLMaJ by decomposing evaluation tasks into simpler sub-tasks. In this work, we systematically compare LLMaJ methods with and without decomposition on multiple NLG datasets. We find no evidence that LLMaJ with task decomposition leads to performance gains over a fair baseline that does not use decomposition. Instead, we find that previously reported performance gains in decomposition-based LLMaJ stem from using human labels as training data, and not task decomposition itself. Also, we find that, when human labels are available, LLMaJ without using task decomposition can perform comparably to human annotators.

</details>


### [210] [Subword Segmental BabyLMs: Learning to Tokenise for Sample-Efficient Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01151)
*Francois Meyer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了2026年BabyLM挑战赛中的两种新型子词分段语言模型：SubSegGPT（解码器-only架构，在自回归预训练中学习分词）和SubSegDeBERTa（编码器架构，联合学习生成和分词掩码词）。SubSegDeBERTa在Strict轨道的零样本评估中表现突出，SubSegGPT在Strict-small轨道上优于基于tokenisation的基线。实验表明可学习的子词分词能提升BabyLM预训练的样本效率，且分词会逐渐收敛到兼顾形态对齐和细粒度切分的子词单元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准语言模型训练流程中，子词分词是预处理步骤，无法根据训练目标进行优化。作者旨在探索子词分段语言建模这一替代范式，即在训练过程中学习分词，让模型自主发现能优化训练目标的子词单元，从而提升样本效率。

Method: 提出两种新的子词分段LM：1) SubSegGPT——解码器-only模型，在自回归预训练期间学习tokenisation；2) SubSegDeBERTa——基于编码器的模型，联合学习生成及分词掩码词。两者均在Strict和Strict-small轨道上训练。

Result: 在Strict轨道上最佳提交为SubSegDeBERTa，在零样本评估中获得显著提升；在Strict-small轨道上最佳提交为SubSegGPT，优于基于tokenisation的基线。分析子词学习动态发现，分词逐渐收敛于平衡形态对齐与细粒度分割的子词单元。

Conclusion: 可学习的子词分词能够提升BabyLM预训练的样本效率，验证了子词分段语言建模作为标准化分词预处理替代方案的有效性。

Abstract: In the standard LM training pipeline, subword tokenisation is applied as a preprocessing step. Subword segmental language modelling is an alternative paradigm in which tokenisation is learned during training, allowing the model to discover subword units that optimise its training objective. In this paper, we present our submission to the 2026 BabyLM Challenge, for which we develop two new subword segmental LMs: SubSegGPT and SubSegDeBERTa. SubSegGPT is a decoder-only model that learns tokenisation during autoregressive pretraining. SubSegDeBERTa is an encoder-based model that jointly learns to generate and tokenise masked words. We train both for the Strict and Strict-small tracks. Our top submission to Strict is SubSegDeBERTa, which achieves notable gains in zero-shot evaluation. Our top submission to Strict-small is SubSegGPT, which outperforms tokenisation-based baselines. Our results show that learnable subword tokenisation can improve sample-efficiency for BabyLM pretraining. We analyse the subword learning dynamics of our models and find that tokenisation gradually converges on subword units that balance morphological alignment and fine-grained segmentation.

</details>


### [211] [LLMPEDIA: Browsing, Verifying, and Comparing the Parametric Encyclopedic Knowledge of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01182)
*Muhammed Saeed,Simon Razniewski*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LLMPEDIA，一个由参记忆递归物化出的开放百科（约130万个词条，来自三个大模型家族），将该知识库中采样出的原子声明与维基百科及精选网络语料校验，发现真实支持率仅68.4%，比MMLU基准低逾21个百分点，其中30.5%的声明因无法裁决而被标记为"证据不足"，揭示了固定问题集基准的可用性偏差及长尾知识覆盖缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旗舰语言模型在MMLU等基准上成绩超过90%，看似饱和，然而这类基准仅能测试实验者预先设想到的问题，存在固定问题集的可用性偏差（availability bias），无法反映模型对海量未问及知识的真实掌握程度，因此需要一种可测量、可浏览的方式来揭示并弥补这一偏差。

Method: 作者从三个模型家族（GPT-5-mini、DeepSeek-V3.2、Llama-3.3-70B）的参记忆中以递归方式物化出约130万个词条（无检索增强），随后对分层抽样的原子声明进行审计，利用维基百科及精选网页语料逐条验证，将每条声明标注为支持、反驳或证据不足三种结论，并借助统一随机样本估算真实的支持率。

Result: 在统一随机样本中，真实支持率为68.4%，比MMLU低超过21个百分点；其中30.5%的声明被判定为"证据不足"，这些断言既无法被基准覆盖，也难以被世界上最大的百科全书中裁判，可能属于长尾知识或看似合理的幻觉，现有证据无法区分，从而将GPTKB对三元组的覆盖缺口扩展到了自由文本。项目以五个一键式视图在线呈现并提供稳定URL。

Conclusion: LLMPEDIA通过大规模递归物化与人工审计，定量揭示了大模型在固定基准之外的显着性能落差和"证据不足"灰区，说明基准高分不能代表模型真实知识水平；它所构建的开放、可逐条检视的在线百科为社区观测模型知识边界提供了新工具。

Abstract: Flagship language models appear saturated on benchmarks like MMLU (Hendrycks et al., 2021), scoring above 90% - yet benchmarks test only what the experimenter thought to ask, the availability bias of fixed question sets. LLMPEDIA makes this bias measurable and browsable. We recursively materialized ~1.3M articles from three model families' parametric memory (GPT-5-mini, DeepSeek-V3.2, Llama-3.3-70B) without retrieval, then audited a stratified sample of atomic claims against Wikipedia and a curated web stack, coloring every claim supported, refuted, or insufficient (Saeed and Razniewski, 2026). On a uniform random sample the true rate is 68.4% - more than 21 pp below MMLU - with 30.5% of claims insufficient: assertions no benchmark probes and the world's largest encyclopedia cannot adjudicate - long-tail knowledge or plausible hallucination, the evidence cannot tell - extending to free text the coverage gap GPTKB established for triples (Hu et al., 2025). The resulting live, open encyclopedia lets visitors inspect this frontier one claim at a time through five one-click views - link-traversal exploration, claim-level factuality, cross-model and political-persona comparison, and a guided topic drill-down - each page, claim, and verdict at a stable URL. LLMPEDIA is live at https://llmpedia.net

</details>


### [212] [PersuaRL: Reinforcement Learning-Driven Multi-Expert Selection for Persuasive Dialogue Generation in Insurance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01188)
*Rohan Kirti,Akash Ghosh,Aryan Vats,Niladri Ghosh,Shipra Shriparn,Roshni Ramnani,Anutosh Maitra,Sriparna Saha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对保险领域中大规模语言模型（LLM）驱动的数字对话代理缺乏说服力的问题，提出了InsureDial数据集和PersuaRL强化学习框架，通过自适应策略选择和协调增强对话说服力，并在人类与自动评估中优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的对话代理虽擅长事实性交流，但在制造与信任、清晰度至关重要的垂直领域（如保险）中缺乏真正有说服力、上下文敏感的对话能力，现有方法无法在动态对话中有效组织和运用说服策略。

Method: 提出PersuaRL：一个基于强化学习的框架，使对话代理能够根据不断演变的对话上下文，自适应地探索、选择并协调多个专家模块中的说服策略。同时构建了InsureDial数据集，专门针对车险交互中的说服性沟通特性进行标注。

Result: 在两个基准说服对话数据集（含InsureDial）上进行了大规模自动评估和人类评估，结果显示PersuaRL在产生上下文适切且高度有说服力的响应方面一致优于基线方法。

Conclusion: PersuaRL通过强化学习驱动的自适应策略协调，显著提升了LLM对话代理在保险场景下的说服能力，验证了其在说服性对话领域的有效性和普适性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are revolutionizing digital communication by powering conversational agents deployed across domains such as customer service, digital sales, and insurance. These agents, built on LLMs, can understand user input, retrieve relevant information, and generate coherent responses. However, while they excel at factual communication, they often lack the ability to engage in truly persuasive, context-sensitive dialogue, especially in domains like insurance, where trust and clarity are critical. Building on this need within the insurance domain, our work focuses on improving the persuasiveness of digital agents, aka LLMs. To support this, we introduce InsureDial, a Persuasive Insurance Dialogue dataset, designed to capture the nuances of persuasive communication specific to motor insurance interactions. We introduce PersuaRL, a reinforcement learning-based framework that equips LLM-driven dialogue agents with the ability to adaptively explore, select, and coordinate strategies across multiple expert modules, guided by the evolving dialogue context, to achieve more effective persuasion. We conduct extensive automatic human and qualitative evaluations on two benchmark persuasion dialogue datasets, including our InsureDial. Our evaluations consistently demonstrate that PersuaRL outperforms baseline, generating contextually appropriate and highly persuasive responses.

</details>


### [213] [CaRL-EM: Cost-Aware Reinforcement Learning for Entity Matching with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01195)
*Chaohui Guo,Michel Klein,Zhisheng Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CaRL-EM，一个基于强化学习的控制器，用于在成本感知的序列决策框架下管理LLM实体匹配操作。该控制器能根据不同任务复杂度自适应选择操作符（Match/Compare/Select/Decide）和模型容量，以最大化质量-成本目标，并在7个基准上实现了比强基线和手动流水线更好的质量-成本权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的实体匹配方法要么进行独立的成对决策，要么依赖手动设计的复合流水线，在多候选现实场景中缺乏灵活性，且通常忽略大规模推理成本问题。该工作旨在解决LLM实体匹配在代价约束下的自适应决策问题。

Method: 将LLM实体匹配形式化为一个成本感知的序列决策问题，设计了一个基于强化学习（RL）的控制器CaRL-EM。控制器利用锚记录、候选集和成本的状态信息，自适应地在不同操作符（Match/Compare/Select/Decide）和不同模型容量之间做出选择。策略通过抽象操作符与环境交互，使得同一控制器可在推理时复用于不同的底层LLM后端而无需重训练。

Result: 在7个基准上的实验表明：CaRL-EM (i) 能根据任务复杂度动态规划廉价和昂贵操作符的使用；(ii) 实现跨多样数据集和领域的稳健零样本迁移；(iii) 相比强LLM基线及手动流水线，始终获得更好的质量-成本权衡，在相同或更高质量下实现更低的推理成本。

Conclusion: CaRL-EM通过强化学习控制器实现了LLM实体匹配中质量与成本的自适应平衡，证明了动态规划算子与模型容量选择的可行性，且策略可与底层LLM后端解耦，具备良好的可迁移性和零样本泛化能力。

Abstract: Entity matching (EM) requires fine-grained contextual understanding and domain knowledge. Recent work shows that large language models (LLMs) can serve as strong matchers across domains, but most methods either make independent pairwise decisions or rely on manually designed composite pipelines, thus lacking flexibility in realistic multi-candidate settings. At the same time, they typically ignore inference cost at scale. We formulate LLM-based EM with candidates as a cost-aware sequential decision problem and propose CaRL-EM, a reinforcement learning controller that manages LLM operations. Given the state of an anchor record, its candidate set, and the cost, CaRL-EM adaptively chooses among different operators (Match/Compare/Select/Decide) and model capacities to maximize a quality-cost objective. The policy interacts with abstract operators, allowing the same controller to be reused with different underlying LLM backends at inference time without retraining. Experiments on 7 benchmarks show that CaRL-EM (i) learns to dynamically plan the usage of inexpensive and expensive operators based on task complexity, (ii) achieves robust zero-shot transfer across diverse datasets and domains, and (iii) consistently achieves a better quality-cost trade-off than strong LLM-based baselines and manually designed pipelines, yielding a lower inference cost at comparable or higher quality.

</details>


### [214] [Towards AI-Assisted Clinical Trial Matching: Practical Considerations, Multicenter Evaluation, and Real-World Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01202)
*Yin Fang,Qiao Jin,Shubo Tian,Lauren He,Maya Geer,Noor Naffakh,Ryan Huu-Tuan Nguyen,Zifeng Wang,Jimeng Sun,Charalampos S. Floudas,James L. Gulley,Kamilia Moalem,Catarina Martins Maia,Amanda Nottke,Juan W. Valle,Melinda Bachini,Lourdes Rocha-Nussbaum,Kari Ramage,Nikita Curry,Megan Barnes,Mandy Mansaray,Darlene Gabeau,Craig E. Grossman,Heath Skinner,Michael Burczynski,NIH-TrialBench Consortium,Zhiyong Lu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TrialGPT 2.0 是一个用于真实肿瘤工作流中部署的AI辅助临床试验推荐系统，不仅评估患者是否符合试验资格，还评估哪些试验值得进一步考虑，并提供结构化可检查的解释。在多中心回顾性队列（288例）中，其在top 10推荐中检索到至少一项临床医生推荐试验的概率约为91%，同时将临床医生筛选时间减少55.0%；在六个月的肉瘤前瞻性评估中，将患者获得临床试验机会的扩展提高了90.9%。作者还发布了NIH-TrialBench数据集以支持科学可复现性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床试验对推进癌症治疗至关重要，但许多试验因患者招募不足而失败。尽管对使用AI辅助患者招募的兴趣日益增长，现有系统大多仅进行资格评估，很少在真实肿瘤工作流中进行评估。本文旨在开发并评估一个面向真实部署的AI辅助临床试验推荐系统。

Method: TrialGPT 2.0系统不仅判断患者是否符合资格，还结合患者的当前临床需求和本地工作流优先级评估哪些试验值得进一步考虑，并提供结构化、可检查的解释供专家审查。研究在多个肿瘤相关的环境中进行了回顾性和前瞻性评估，涵盖政府、学术癌症中心、患者倡导和NIH转诊工作流。

Result: 在多中心回顾性队列（288例）中，TrialGPT 2.0在top 10推荐中对约91%的病例检索到至少一项临床医生推荐的试验，并将临床医生筛选时间减少55.0%。在纳入主动精准肿瘤委员会六个月的前瞻性评估中，TrialGPT 2.0提供了常规工作流遗漏的额外试验机会，将患者获得临床试验参与机会扩展了90.9%。此外引入了NIH-TrialBench数据集，包含来自11个NIH研究所和中心的126个多样化合成患者小插图及匹配场景。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，AI通过提高临床医生效率并识别经常被忽视的试验机会，能够辅助临床试验匹配，最终有助于扩大和加速癌症试验的患者入组。

Abstract: Clinical trials are essential for advancing cancer care and drug development, but many fail because of insufficient patient enrollment. While there is growing interest in using AI to support patient recruitment, existing systems largely perform eligibility assessment alone and have rarely been evaluated in real-world oncology workflows. Here we present TrialGPT 2.0, an AI-assisted clinical trial recommendation system designed for real-world deployment. Rather than asking only whether a patient may qualify, the system also assesses which trials warrant further consideration given the patient's current clinical needs and local workflow priorities, and provides structured, inspectable explanations for expert review. Importantly, we evaluated TrialGPT 2.0 retrospectively and prospectively across multiple oncology-focused settings, spanning government, academic cancer-center, patient-advocacy, and NIH referral workflows. In retrospective multicenter cohorts comprising 288 cases, TrialGPT 2.0 retrieved at least one clinician-recommended trial in its top 10 recommendations for approximately 91% of cases while reducing clinician screening time by 55.0%. In a six-month prospective evaluation embedded in an active precision oncology tumor board, TrialGPT 2.0 contributed additional trial opportunities missed by the routine workflow, expanding patient access to clinical trial participation by 90.9%. To support scientific reproducibility, we also introduce NIH-TrialBench, a clinician-authored dataset comprising 126 diverse synthetic patient vignettes and matching scenarios from 11 NIH Institutes and Centers. Together, these results support the value of AI to assist clinical trial matching by improving clinician efficiency and identifying frequently overlooked trial opportunities, ultimately helping to expand and accelerate accrual to cancer trials.

</details>


### [215] [Ready to Speak: Aligning LLMs for TTS-Friendly Text Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01246)
*Thibaut Thonet,Jos Rozen,Laurent Besacier*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究如何让大语言模型直接生成适合TTS（文本转语音）播报的文本，将其建模为偏好对齐问题，引入CORA和Recipe两个偏好数据集，并提出包含启发式指标、TTS→ASR评估流水线和MUSHRA人工听测的评估套件。实验表明，基于可解释特征的FaST框架在TTS友好性与有用性之间取得了最佳平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM主要以书面文本为优化目标，其输出虽然语法正确、具有帮助性，但往往不适合通过TTS进行语音播报。现有方法依赖下游重写模块，缺乏让LLM原生生成TTS友好文本的对齐机制。本文旨在将TTS友好文本生成形式化为偏好对齐问题，直接优化LLM而非依赖事后改写。

Method: 1) 构建两个覆盖不同目标领域的偏好数据集CORA和Recipe，包含配对的TTS友好与不友好回复；2) 提出由三部分组成的评估套件：基于模式的启发式指标、TTS→ASR自动评估流水线、以及MUSHRA人工听测试验；3) 将FaST（特征感知采样与调优）框架与其他多种对齐基线方法在TTS友好生成任务上进行对比实验。

Result: FaST框架在多个设置下在TTS友好性与帮助性之间取得了最佳综合权衡；此外，不同评估指标之间呈现强相关性，表明可以通过高效的启发式指标可靠地评估TTS友好性。

Conclusion: 将LLM直接对齐以生成TTS友好文本是可行的，FaST作为一种利用可解释特征替代黑盒奖励模型的对齐方法在该任务上表现优异。同时，启发式指标与更复杂的评估方法高度相关，为TTS友好性的可靠便捷评估提供了支持。

Abstract: Current Large Language Models (LLMs) are primarily optimized for written text, often producing outputs that are grammatically correct and helpful yet poorly suited for spoken delivery via Text-to-Speech (TTS). In this work, we study how to make LLMs natively generate TTS-friendly text, which we frame as a preference alignment problem: instead of relying on downstream rewriting modules, we directly align LLMs to generate text optimized for spoken delivery. We introduce two preference datasets spanning different target domains, CORA and Recipe, which contain paired TTS-friendly and TTS-unfriendly responses. We further propose an evaluation suite combining a pattern-based heuristic metric, a TTS$\to$ASR evaluation pipeline, and a MUSHRA listening study with human judges. Our experiments compare the recently proposed Feature-aware Sampling and Tuning (FaST) framework -- leveraging interpretable features instead of a black-box reward model -- against an array of alignment baselines on the TTS-friendly generation task. Notably, we found that FaST achieves the best overall tradeoff between TTS-friendliness and helpfulness across various settings. We also identified a strong correlation between our different metrics, highlighting the ability to reliably assess TTS-friendliness via an efficient heuristic.

</details>


### [216] [From Base Rollouts to RL Reasoning: A Budgeted Search Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01274)
*Wenhe Sun,Cunxiang Wang,Zijun Yao,Yixin Cao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过统一解码框架（UDF）和行为分析研究RLVR提升语言模型推理能力的机制，发现RL调制后的默认策略曲线可被基础模型在搜索下的结构化路径近似，并提出"受约束的预算工作点转移规则"（BOPTR），支持"内部化搜索"解读——大部分RL增益源于采样效率向基础模型已可到达的工作点的转移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR能提升语言模型推理能力，但此类提升与推理时解码/搜索之间的关系尚不清楚：RL究竟是创造了基础模型所不具备的新推理能力，还是仅仅将rollout分布转移到基础模型已能生成但很少采样的轨迹上？理解这一问题对模型训练与推理设计至关重要。

Method: 提出统一解码框架（UDF），将token级采样、beam-like搜索、树搜索和序列级重采样统一表达为共享预算操作空间上的可执行策略，并以pass@k、self-consistency、best-of-N和首次完成成功率从事后评分。使用SimpleRL-Zoo配对的Base/RL检查点，检验RL默认策略曲线是否可被Base操作点（在搜索下）的结构化路径近似，拟合出BOPTR幂律关系 N_Base≈αN_RL^β，并测试其对未见模型、未见基准及无RL监督情形的泛化性。

Result: 在Math500、AIME、GPQA和IFEval上，pass@k恢复路径遵循BOPTR规则（基准条件指数）。Qwen2.5-7B上BOPTR在非oracle规则中迁移误差最低（3.41pp，95%CI [2.32,5.53]）；三种子复现为3.07±0.39pp。规则可扩展到四个系列共10个模型（拟合后新增检查点误差3.28-4.87pp）、四个未参与拟合的基准（5.03pp vs 拟合内4.44pp），在无目标模型RL检查点（4.19pp）及完全无RL监督（5.08pp）情形下依然成立。

Conclusion: 结果支持一种有条件的"内部化搜索"解读：在所测配方下，测量的RL增益大部分对应于采样效率向基础模型在搜索下已能到达的工作点的转变。但作者将该缩放模式视为对该配方与模型群体的描述性发现，报告其失效情形，并强调UDF与BOPTR仅作为行为诊断工具，而非参数级等价性的证据。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves language-model reasoning, but how these gains relate to inference-time decoding and search remains unclear. Does RL create reasoning the base model lacks, or shift the rollout distribution toward trajectories it can already reach but rarely samples? We study this behaviorally with a Unified Decoding Framework (UDF), which expresses token-level sampling, beam-like search, tree search, and sequence-level resampling as executable policies over a shared budgeted operating space, scored post hoc with pass@$k$, self-consistency, best-of-$N$, and first-finish success. Using paired Base/RL checkpoints from SimpleRL-Zoo, we ask whether an RL default-policy curve can be approximated by a structured path of Base operating points. On Math500, AIME, GPQA, and IFEval, the pass@$k$ recovery path follows a Budgeted Operating-Point Transition Rule (BOPTR), $N_{\mathrm{Base}} \approx αN_{\mathrm{RL}}^β$, with benchmark-conditioned exponents. On Qwen2.5-7B, BOPTR gives the lowest transfer error among the non-oracle rules we test, 3.41 pp (95% CI [2.32, 5.53]); a three-seed replication gives 3.07 $\pm$ 0.39 pp. The rule extends to ten models across four families (3.28 to 4.87 pp on checkpoints added after fitting), to four benchmarks it was never fitted on (5.03 pp vs. 4.44 pp in fit), and holds without an RL checkpoint for the target model (4.19 pp) or without RL supervision of any kind (5.08 pp). These results support a qualified internalized-search reading: under the recipe we test, much of the measured RL gain corresponds to a change in sampling efficiency toward operating points the base model can already reach under search. We treat the scaling patterns as descriptive of this recipe and cohort, report where they break down, and use UDF and BOPTR as behavioral diagnostics rather than evidence of parameter-level equivalence.

</details>


### [217] [Some Emotions Run Deeper: Layer-wise Probing and Causal Intervention in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01279)
*Tian Fang,Gaël Guibon,Davide Buscaldi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨了情感信息在不同LLM层中的可访问性是否取决于文本来源。跨三个数据集（Twitter、Reddit、自传叙述）和八个1B-9B开源LLM，结合层次探测、离线特征缩放、在线前向干预、迁移分析及早退分类器，发现最佳探测层随语料库系统性移动，且探测选层干预对准确率影响显著大于随机波段，所选波段在数据集和情感类别间可迁移，早退表征平均优于全深度6.9个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对LLM中情感分层分析大多基于单一语料库，无法区分"情感在模型某一深度变得可访问"究竟是模型自身的属性，还是也取决于文本来源（如情感表达的显式程度和情境化程度）。作者希望跨多个不同情感表达方式的语料库来检验这一假设，从而更好地理解情感信息在模型内部表征中的分布机制。

Method: 使用三个数据集（Twitter帖子、Reddit评论、自传叙述）涵盖从显式词汇线索到深层情境推理的情感表达谱系；八个1B-9B开源模型（Llama、Qwen、Granite系列）。采用组合方法：逐层探测（layer-wise probing）、按标签长度分箱匹配分布的离线特征缩放、在线前向干预（在前向传播中对探测选定的层间波段进行干预）、跨数据集和情感类别的迁移分析、以及基于探测选定表征的早退分类器。

Result: (i) 最佳探测层随语料库系统性变化，从输入邻近层到超过模型半深度，且在控制标签长度分布后该排序依然成立；(ii) 前向干预中，探测选定的波段比同宽度随机波段对测试准确率的降低多5-6个百分点（q<0.01）；(iii) 选定波段跨数据集和情感类别可迁移，暗示共享的情感信息底物而非严格逐情感特异的表征；(iv) 探测选定的早退表征平均比全深度退出高6.9个百分点。

Conclusion: 情感信息在LLM中的层间定位并非单纯的模型属性，而是受文本来源中情感表达方式（显式程度与情境化程度）的显著调节。这一发现对高效情感分类（早退策略）和模型干预（定向编辑）具有实际意义，表明需要根据输入类型动态调整情感信息的提取层。

Abstract: Emotion is expressed in text along a wide spectrum, from surface lexical cues to inferences entangled with content. Most layer-wise analyses of emotion in LLMs use a single corpus, leaving open whether the depth at which emotion becomes accessible is a property of the model or also of the text source. We investigate this across three datasets spanning different degrees of explicitness and contextualization in emotion expression (Twitter posts, Reddit comments, and autobiographical narratives) and eight 1B--9B open-weight LLMs from the Llama, Qwen, and Granite families. We combine layer-wise probing with offline feature scaling and online forward interventions, transfer analyses, and an early-exit classifier. We find that (i) the best probing layer shifts systematically across corpora, from input-adjacent layers to over half model depth, and this ordering persists after matching label-by-length-bin distributions; (ii) across the evaluated settings, forward-pass interventions on probe-selected bands reduce test accuracy by 5--6 points more than same-width random bands ($q < 0.01$); (iii) selected bands transfer across datasets and emotion categories, suggesting partially shared affective information rather than strictly per-emotion substrates; and (iv) probe-selected early-exit representations outperform full-depth exits by $6.9$ percentage points on average.

</details>


### [218] [Explore Before Committing: Hypothesis-Guided Search for Deep Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01294)
*Ruochen Zhou,Zhengyu Chen,Luan Zhang,Siyang Gao,Yee Whye Teh,Shiqi Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出HypoSearch方法，通过轻量级假设作为软搜索提示、有界独立分支探索和跨分支证据比较，解决深度研究代理在单条演化轨迹中过早陷入误导方向的问题，在四个深度研究基准和三个骨干模型上一致优于单轨迹搜索和标准并行基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度研究代理在回答复杂问题时常常沿着单一演化轨迹进行搜索。轨迹级分析揭示了一个常见失败模式：代理在早期遇到多个可行方向的搜索状态时，往往会先跟随一个方向而未能收集足够的比较性证据，且后续工具调用会强化同一路径，导致初始方向误导时失败概率增加。

Method: HypoSearch方法包含三个核心组件：(1)生成轻量级假设作为软搜索提示，将模糊探索具象化为候选方向；(2)通过有界独立分支并行探索这些假设方向；(3)在承诺某个方向之前进行分支级证据比较。此外，还通过行为信号的监督微调来筛选紧凑训练轨迹。

Result: 在四个深度研究基准和三个骨干模型上，HypoSearch一致优于单轨迹搜索和标准并行基线。在BC-small任务上将Qwen3.5-122B从46.7提升至60.0，且工具调用次数少于五条独立轨迹。监督微调研究表明行为信号能筛选紧凑训练轨迹并减少未过滤数据的性能退化。

Conclusion: 成功的深度研究轨迹通过两种行为降低风险：将模糊探索扎根于具体候选对象，以及在当前路径薄弱或不完整时切换方向。基于这些发现的HypoSearch验证了假设驱动、分支比较的搜索策略能有效提升深度研究代理性能。

Abstract: Deep-research agents answer complex questions by interacting with search and browsing tools, yet they often search along a single evolving trajectory. Our trajectory-level analysis reveals a common failure mode in which the agent may encounter an early search state with several plausible directions, but follow one direction before collecting enough comparative evidence. Once this happens, subsequent tool calls tend to reinforce the same path, increasing the chance of failure when the initial direction is misleading. We further find that successful trajectories reduce this risk through two behaviors: grounding vague exploration in concrete candidates and shifting directions when the current path is weak or incomplete. Based on these findings, we propose HypoSearch, which generates lightweight hypotheses as soft search hints, explores them through bounded independent branches, and compares branch-level evidence before commitment. Across four deep-research benchmarks and three backbone models, HypoSearch consistently outperforms single-trajectory search and standard parallel baselines, improving Qwen3.5-122B from 46.7 to 60.0 on BC-small while using fewer tool calls than five independent trajectories. A pilot supervised fine-tuning study further shows that these behavioral signals can curate compact training trajectories and reduce degradation from unfiltered data.

</details>


### [219] [Exploring Sparse Autoencoders in Text-Based Causal Confounding Adjustment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01322)
*Mian Zhong,Katherine A. Keith,Anjalie Field*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于稀疏自编码器（SAE）的文本混杂因素调整管线，通过条件独立性检验迭代选择最小SAE特征集，在双值混杂因子的半合成评估中比替代表示方法获得更低的偏差和更高的覆盖率，并引入更真实的多标签数据混杂评估场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于文本数据的因果推断需调整文本中的混杂信息，但存在表示选择的权衡：表示需足够大/稠密以保留混杂变量实现无偏估计，同时需足够小/稀疏以满足有限样本重叠并降低估计方差。

Method: 提出一种新的因果调整管线，使用稀疏自编码器（SAEs）得到文本表示，并通过条件独立性检验迭代地选择最小SAE特征子集用于调整，以平衡偏差和方差。

Result: 在含有双值混杂因子的标准半合成评估中，SAE表示较其他替代表示取得更低的偏差和更高的覆盖率；同时引入基于多标签数据的更真实半合成评估，显示现成的调整方法在更复杂场景下需要进一步研究。

Conclusion: SAE表示在文本混杂调整中优于现有替代方案，且其特征的可解释性为因果推断中的证伪检验提供了机会；但复杂多标签混杂场景下现有方法仍需改进。

Abstract: In many settings, studying causal questions based on text data requires adjusting for confounding information within texts. Yet there is a tradeoff in constructing text representations for adjustment: they must be sufficiently large and/or dense to preserve the confounding variables necessary for unbiased effect estimation, but sufficiently small and/or sparse to satisfy finite-sample overlap and yield low-variance estimates. To address this tradeoff, we turn to sparse autoencoders (SAEs), and propose a novel causal adjustment pipeline that iteratively selects a minimal set of SAE features via conditional independence tests. We find that SAE representations achieve better adjustments (lower bias and and higher coverage) than alternative representations in standard semi-synthetic evaluations with binary confounders, and their interpretability offers opportunities for falsification. We also introduce a more realistic semi-synthetic evaluation that uses multi-label data as the unobserved confounders and find off-the-shelf adjustment methods require increased investigation for these more complex settings. Code: https://github.com/mianzg/sae-text-confounder

</details>


### [220] [VerTox: Verifiable Reward-Guided Corpus Poisoning Against Neural Ranking Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01325)
*Zhiqi Huang,Vivek Datla,Zhichao Xu,Puxuan Yu,Vivek Srikumar,Alfy Samuel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出VerTox框架，首次将针对神经排序模型的语料库投毒攻击建模为可验证奖励引导的强化学习（RLVR）问题，通过将排序扭曲与事实破坏耦合的奖励塑形，微调小型LLM生成对抗性文档，在不被检测的前提下实现近乎完美的攻击成功率，并显著降低下游RAG应用的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经排序模型是现代信息检索系统和RAG流水线的核心组件，但其在大语言模型能够生成流畅且具有欺骗性内容的时代下的鲁棒性尚未被充分理解。现有研究缺乏对语料库投毒攻击的系统性框架，无法有效评估和暴露排序模型的脆弱性。

Method: 提出VerTox框架，将语料库投毒视为一个可验证奖励引导的强化学习（RLVR）问题。通过专门设计的奖励塑形机制将排序扭曲与事实破坏显式耦合，微调小型语言模型使其成为对抗性文档生成器，在保持文档流畅性和低困惑度的同时最大化攻击效果。

Result: 实验表明该方法在不同主流神经排序架构以及专有商业嵌入模型上均取得近乎完美的攻击成功率，生成的对抗性文档经常排名高于目标文档。此外，这些文档具有流畅性和低困惑度，难以被检测，并且显著降低了下游RAG应用的性能。

Conclusion: 神经排序模型在LLM时代的语料库投毒攻击面前表现出显著的脆弱性，VerTox证明了将排序扭曲与事实破坏结合的投毒攻击对IR系统和RAG应用构成严重威胁，需要更强大的防御机制来应对此类攻击。

Abstract: Neural ranking models have become core components of modern information retrieval systems and important building blocks of AI systems such as retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) pipelines. However, their robustness remains insufficiently understood in the presence of large language models (LLMs), which can generate fluent and deceptive content at scale. This work investigates the vulnerability of neural ranking models to corpus poisoning attacks, in which an adversary injects a small number of maliciously crafted documents into the corpus to distort ranking behavior. We propose VerTox, the first framework to formulate corpus poisoning as a verifiable reward-guided reinforcement learning (RLVR) problem. By explicitly coupling ranking distortion with factual corruption through specialized reward shaping, we fine-tune compact LLMs into adversarial generators. Experiments demonstrate that our method achieves near-perfect attack success rates, producing adversarial documents that frequently rank higher than target documents across major neural ranking architectures, as well as a proprietary commercial embedding model. The generated adversarial documents are fluent and exhibit low perplexity, making them difficult to detect. Furthermore, by explicitly encouraging factual corruption, our adversarial documents significantly degrade the performance of a downstream RAG application.

</details>


### [221] [Probing Factual Knowledge Transfer with Training Data Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01341)
*Romina Oji,Marc Braun,Marcel Bollmann,Marco Kuhlmann,Jenny Kunz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过干预式框架研究了多语言模型在持续预训练中是否跨语言迁移事实知识。研究发现事实迁移非常有限，多数英语习得的事实无法迁移到波斯语；并揭示了负样本候选集难度和源语言实体频率对迁移评估的重要影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言模型在持续预训练中是否真正跨语言迁移事实知识，还是主要从目标语言数据中直接记忆？此前的评估方法难以可靠区分这两种情况，需要更加严谨的干预式实验设计来回答。

Method: 提出基于干预的框架：从英语预训练模型出发，在与训练数据中系统性移除特定事实（按不同粒度）的波斯语数据上继续预训练。构建SIFT资源（500个三元组、20种关系），按事实主体的文化来源分为通用实体和波斯相关实体，并设计原生波斯语完形填空模板用于移除与评估。

Result: 在最严格的移除条件下，绝大多数英语习得的事实未能迁移到波斯语，表明事实迁移非常有限。句子级共现移除不足以消除事实信号；随机选择的较易负样本会通过浅层联想启发式夸大表观迁移，而在更难候选集上表现大幅降低。此外，源语言实体频率影响显著，波斯相关事实在英语语料中罕见，几乎不迁移。

Conclusion: 多语言模型在持续预训练中的跨语言事实迁移能力十分有限，事实知识主要依赖目标语言直接数据。评估框架必须考虑移除粒度和负样本难度等因素，否则容易高估迁移能力。

Abstract: Do multilingual language models transfer factual knowledge across languages during continued pretraining, or do they mostly recall facts learned directly from the target-language data? To answer this question more reliably, we propose an intervention-based framework: starting from an English-pretrained model, we continue pretraining on Persian data from which specific facts have been systematically removed at varying levels of granularity. We construct SIFT, a resource of 500 triples across 20 relations, stratified by the cultural origin of each fact's subject into general (globally prominent) and Persian-related entities, designed for both systematic fact removal from training data and evaluation, with natively written Persian cloze templates. Our results show that fact transfer is very limited: under the strictest removal condition, a large majority of English-acquired facts fail to transfer into Persian. We further show that sentence-level co-occurrence removal is insufficient to eliminate fact signal, and that easier (randomly selected) negative candidate sets substantially inflate apparent transfer by rewarding shallow associative heuristics, while performance on a harder candidate set that allows for less reliance on heuristics is much lower. Finally, we show that source-language entity frequency has a large influence, with Persian-related facts, which are orders of magnitude rarer in the English corpus, hardly transferring.

</details>


### [222] [CHARM: Character Hallucination for Multicultural Role Play Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01352)
*Sunkyung Han,Nahyeon Park,Gaeun Seo,Seunghyun Yoon,JinYeong Bak*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CHARM基准，一个包含40个来自五个文化语言区域角色的多文化基准，用于区分角色扮演LLM的越界幻觉是源于边界认知失败还是认知但未能遵从；通过对六个LLM的评估发现，幻觉主要由遵从失败驱动——模型常常识别出查询超出角色知识却仍给出越界的真实答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估能检测角色幻觉，但很少区分错误是源于未能识别知识边界，还是虽识别了边界却未能遵从。这种区分对理解角色扮演LLM失败的根本机制至关重要。

Method: 构建CHARM多文化基准，包含40个真实与虚构角色（来自五个文化语言区域），由母语评审验证；设计两类边界探测：时间边界（历史vs现代）和跨宇宙边界（角色叙事或历史宇宙之外的实体）；使用可弃权的多项选择题；提出两阶段评估框架，分别测度边界认知（明确识别查询越界）与边界遵从（回答具体问题时弃权）；对六个LLM进行评测。

Result: 六个LLM的评估显示幻觉主要由遵从失败驱动。模型经常承认查询超出角色知识范围，但依然给出事实性的、越出角色的回答。通过将相同问题重投给目标角色，确认大部分这类案例是参数化覆盖——模型存有相关事实但未能抑制输出。同时观察到系统性文化差异，与不同地区角色在模型知识中代表性的不平衡一致。

Conclusion: 角色幻觉的核心瓶颈不在边界认知而在遵从环节——模型"知道"但不"抑制"；这为提升角色扮演LLM的忠实性提供了明确优化方向（加强角色约束的执行机制），并揭示了跨文化表征不平衡带来的系统性偏差。

Abstract: Role-playing large language models (LLMs) are expected to adopt a character's style while also respecting that character's knowledge boundaries. Prior evaluations detect character hallucination but rarely distinguish whether errors arise from failure to recognize a boundary or from failure to comply despite recognition. We introduce CHARM, a multicultural benchmark of 40 real and fictional characters drawn from five cultural-linguistic regions, and validated by native reviewers. It probes two boundary types, Temporal (historical vs. modern) and Cross-Universe (entities outside a character's narrative or historical universe), using abstention-enabled multiple-choice questions. We propose a two-stage evaluation that separates Boundary-Awareness (explicit recognition that a query is out of scope) from Boundary-Compliance (abstention when answering concrete questions). Evaluations across six LLMs show that hallucination is driven predominantly by compliance failures. Models frequently acknowledge that a query lies outside the character's knowledge yet still provide factual, out-of-character answers. By re-posing the same questions to the target character, we confirm that a large fraction of these cases are verified parametric overrides; the model stores the relevant fact but fails to suppress it. We also observe systematic cultural variation in these failures, consistent with imbalances in how characters from different regions are represented in model knowledge.

</details>


### [223] [Where the Verifier Fails: A Category-Level Audit of Reward Signals in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01354)
*Esther Xin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过变形测试（metamorphic testing）系统性地分解了强化学习可验证奖励（RLVR）与基准评估中自动验证器的错误来源。作者对四个广泛使用的验证器在307,420个判决上进行测试，发现：验证器自验证率从53.8%到95.2%不等；错误集中分布在空白符和标点（占93.0%）；不同验证器虽总错误率相似但失败原因相反；参考数值级联存在尺度不变的相对容差问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究仅报告验证器的总体验收率（约94%）并将失败归因于LaTeX解析，但缺乏对错误预算的具体分解。作者希望精确量化不同答案形式对验证器失败的贡献，从而揭示验证器的真实可靠性问题。

Method: 将变形测试应用于验证器而非模型：生成经认证的等价答案变体（即通过构造保持数学意义的改写），使任何拒绝都成为无需人工裁决的、可证明的假阴性。然后测量四个广泛使用的验证器在不同答案类别下的拒绝率，共计307,420个判决。

Result: 三个关键发现：(1) 同一验证器自验证率从53.8%到95.2%不等（相差41.3个百分点），同一库的不同配置对49.9%的配对存在分歧；(2) 错误并非分散在各解析类别，而集中于空白符和标点，占默认LaTeX配置合同失败（in contract failures）的93.0%，尾随句点或换行是主要错误来源；(3) 将拒绝与执行失败分离后发现，总错误率相似的验证器失败原因可能相反；参考数值级联以尺度不变的相对容差作为阶跃函数接受偏离正确的答案——10^4以下接受率为0%，以上为100%。

Conclusion: 自动验证器的可靠性被严重高估，单点自验证率具有高度误导性。错误主要源自空白符和标点而非深层解析问题，这提示修复优先级应聚焦格式规范。数值级联的尺度不变容差说明验证器存在根本性设计缺陷，需要重新审视验证机制的设计与基准评估的公平性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) and standard benchmark evaluation both rely on an automatic verifier that turns a free text answer into a binary reward. Prior work reports that one evaluation harness accepts only about 94% of its own ground truth answers, blaming LaTeX parsing. That is an aggregate: it does not say which answer forms consume the error budget. We supply the decomposition. We apply metamorphic testing to the verifier rather than the model, generating certified equivalent answer variants, that is, rewrites that preserve mathematical meaning by construction, so that any rejection is a provable false negative needing no human adjudication. We then measure rejection per answer category across four widely used verifiers over 307,420 verdicts. We find three things. (1) Self validation ranges from 53.8% to 95.2% on identical inputs, a spread of 41.3 points. The published figure describes one implementation, not the task; two configurations of the same library disagree on 49.9% of pairs. (2) The residual is not spread across parsing categories but concentrated in whitespace and punctuation, which account for 93.0% of in contract failures for the default LaTeX configuration. A trailing period or newline dominates the budget. (3) Separating rejection from execution failure shows that verifiers with similar aggregate error fail for opposite reasons, and that a reference numeric cascade accepts off by one wrong answers as a step function of magnitude, from 0% below 10^4 to 100% at or above, because its relative tolerance is scale invariant.

</details>


### [224] [Separating Syntax from Language: A Mechanistic Account of Translation in Multilingual LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01356)
*Mikhail Sonkin,Tanja Baeumel,Daniil Gurgurov,Josef van Genabith,Simon Ostermann*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过构造控制数据集、因果干预和探针分析，揭示多语言大模型（mLLM）的机器翻译过程比以往认为的更加模块化：输出语言生成不仅与概念内容分离，还进一步可分解为句法结构构建和表层语言实现两个独立阶段，模型先确定目标语言的词序（句法），再实现目标语言表面形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管mLLM在机器翻译上表现优异，但对其跨语言表征转换机制的认知仍不完整。先前研究提出翻译可分解为概念表征和语言形式生成，本文旨在进一步探索这一分解的粒度，验证语言输出阶段是否还能细分，从而更深入地理解翻译的内部工作机制。

Method: 研究者构造了控制性多语言数据集，以隔离跨语言间的词序差异（语法层面）与表层语言差异；使用因果干预（causal interventions）和探针分析（probing）来追踪翻译过程中表征的变换路径，并识别对不同处理阶段敏感的结构组件。

Result: 研究发现模型在翻译时首先构建目标端的词序（句法结构），之后才实现目标语言的表层形式。进一步识别出对句法变换选择性敏感、但对语言身份保持不变的注意力头，表明存在功能上独立的句法commitment阶段。

Conclusion: 该结果确立了句法结构的承诺（commitment）作为翻译中一个独立阶段的存在，扩展了先前对翻译分解的理解，展示了mLLM中翻译由功能不同的组件按顺序实现的机制。

Abstract: Multilingual large language models (mLLMs) achieve strong performance in machine translation, yet our understanding of the mechanisms by which they transform representations from one language to another remains incomplete. Prior work suggests that translation decomposes into separable processes within an mLLM, where conceptual content is first represented independently, followed by a production into language-specific form. In this work, we show that translation is even more modular than previously assumed and that the output language production in translation processes is actually further separable into a syntax and a surface language process. We construct controlled multilingual datasets that isolate cross-linguistic differences in word-order and use causal interventions and probing to track how representations are transformed during translation. We find that models first construct target-side word-order before realizing the target language surface form. We identify individual attention heads that are selectively sensitive to syntactic transformations while remaining largely invariant to language identity. These results establish the commitment to a syntactic structure as an independent stage in translation, extending prior decompositions and showing how translation is implemented by functionally different components within mLLMs.

</details>


### [225] [Investigating Linear Probe Robustness to Linguistic Register, Medical Specialty, and Corpus Shifts in Medical QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01361)
*Nishant Mishra,Ameen Abu-Hanna,Iacer Calixto*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文系统解耦了影响LLM隐藏状态中"truth direction"（线性探针用于检测事实错误的几何方向）泛化能力的三个变量：写作风格（register）、医学领域（specialty）和语料库（corpus）。基于500个MedQA条目构建的基准（每种改写为四种风格）和三个考试语料库上的实验表明，truth direction对写作风格和医学专科基本鲁棒（AUROC下降约0.10和0.03），但在跨语料库迁移时显著退化（MMLU-medical下降0.12，MedMCQA下降0.21），且问题格式无法解释这种退化，表明线性探针捕获的信号部分与数据集结构绑定而非纯粹的医学知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究对于线性探针（linear probes）发现的"truth direction"是否能跨输入变化泛化存在争议，但争议难以解释是因为跨数据集探针迁移实验同时混淆了多种输入变化。作者希望隔离这些变量，分别考察写作风格、医学专科和语料库三个因素各自对truth direction泛化能力的影响，以理解线性探针所捕获的信号本质。

Method: 构建一个基准：取500个MedQA条目，每个改写为四种风格（教科书、患者、临床笔记、口语化），标注临床专科，并与其他两个考试语料库（MedMCQA和MMLUM-medical）分组用于跨数据集评估。对四个开源LLM（2-8B参数）进行探测，分别测量不同风格、不同专科和不同语料库之间的AUROC差值（Δ_register、Δ_specialty），并验证结果的可复制性（第二种生成器及人类撰写的患者问题）。

Result: Truth direction对写作风格大致鲁棒（平均Δ_register≈0.10 AUROC），对医学专科高度鲁棒（Δ_specialty≈0.03），但对语料库变化退化不均匀：MMLU-medical降低0.12 AUROC，MedMCQA降低0.21 AUROC，后者约为写作风格差距的两倍。写作风格的结果在第二种生成器和人类撰写的患者问题上得到复现。

Conclusion: Truth direction在医学领域内大体稳定，但会在某些语料库转换下失效，且问题格式（写作风格）不能解释这种失效。这表明线性探针恢复的信号部分绑定于数据集结构而非纯粹的医学知识，警示跨数据集的探针泛化评估需要解耦多种混淆因素。

Abstract: Linear classifiers trained on hidden states of a large language model (LLM), linear probes, can flag factual errors from a single forward pass. Geometrically, that implies that true and false statements separate along a stable direction in hidden state space, i.e., the truth direction. Prior work disagrees on whether this generalises across input shifts, but the disagreement is hard to interpret because cross-dataset probe transfer experiments confound several kinds of input change at once. We isolate three such variables in medical question-answering (QA): writing style (register), domain (medical specialty), and corpus (dataset). We build a benchmark using 500 MedQA entries, each rewritten into four styles (textbook, patient, clinical note, colloquial), annotated with clinical specialty, and grouped with two other exam corpora, MedMCQA and MMLU-medical, for cross-dataset evaluation. Probing four open-weight LLMs (2--8B), we find that the truth direction is largely robust to writing style (mean $Δ_\text{register} \approx 0.10$ AUROC on held-out facts) and to medical specialty ($Δ_\text{specialty} \approx 0.03$), but degrades unevenly across corpora: by $0.12$ AUROC on MMLU-medical and by $0.21$ on MedMCQA, roughly twice the register gap. The register result replicates with a second generator and carries over to human-written patient questions. The truth direction is therefore largely stable within the medical domain but breaks under some corpus shifts, and question format does not explain the break, which suggests that the signal a linear probe recovers is partly bound to dataset structure rather than to medical knowledge alone.

</details>


### [226] [How Correct Is Your Answer? A Semantic Correctness Framework for Open QA Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01369)
*Elitsa Yotkova,Violeta Kastreva,Petar Velkov,Hristo Boyanov,Dimitar Dimitrov,Ivan Koychev,Preslav Nakov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对开放式问答正确性评估的瓶颈问题，提出了三项可复用贡献：一个八分类的语义正确性分类体系（区分冗长但正确与包含幻觉内容污染的答案）、两个基准数据集（CAP-Correctness含8.8k示例、CAP-Statements含11k示例用于NLI训练）、以及一种基于双向NLI的引用式评估指标CAP（Context-Aware Precision）。实验表明CAP在单调性协议下优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估方法（基于判断和基于相似度）无法有效区分开放式回答中不同类型的失败模式（如不完整、矛盾、过度生成、认可虚假前提等），且常将多种错误混为一谈，缺乏细粒度的语义正确性评估框架。

Method: 1) 提出语义正确性分类体系，将开放式答案划分为八个有序类别；2) 发布CAP-Correctness（8.8k示例，涵盖广泛使用的QA数据集）和CAP-Statements（11k示例，用于将QA对转为陈述句以支持NLI训练）两个基准数据集；3) 提出CAP指标，使用双向NLI对问题条件化的陈述进行评分。

Result: 在单调性协议（验证指标是否遵循分类体系预定排序）下，CAP指标优于已有的各类基线评估方法。

Conclusion: 通过细粒度的语义分类体系、大规模基准数据和基于双向NLI的CAP指标，本文为开放式问答的正确性评估提供了更可靠且可区分不同失败模式的解决方案。

Abstract: Reliable evaluation of open-ended question answering remains a bottleneck for measuring answer correctness of modern LLMs. Unlike multiple-choice tasks, free-form answers may be correct in many surface forms and may fail in qualitatively different ways, including incompleteness, contradiction, overgeneration, and endorsement of false premises. Existing judgment-based and similarity-based metrics often collapse these distinctions. We address this gap with three reusable contributions. First, we introduce a semantic correctness taxonomy that assigns open-ended answers to eight ordered classes, separating verbose-but-correct answers from those contaminated by hallucinated content. Second, we release CAP-Correctness, an 8.8k-example benchmark spanning widely used QA datasets, and CAP-Statements, an 11k-example dataset for converting question-answer pairs into declarative statements for natural language inference (NLI) training and statement-based evaluation. Third, we introduce CAP (Context-Aware Precision), a reference-based metric that scores question-conditioned statements using bidirectional NLI. Under a monotonicity protocol testing whether metrics respect the taxonomy's intended ordering, CAP outperforms established baselines.

</details>


### [227] [Behaviorally Effective LoRA Writes Are Sparse and Structured](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01374)
*Haruto Sato,Yuki Tanaka,Ren Nakamura,Aoi Kobayashi,Mei Ito*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了LoRA低秩更新中行为的真实分布位置。作者发现行为有效的LoRA写入是稀疏且结构化的，远低于原始低秩参数化所暗示的集中度。通过提出Learned-Basis LoRA（一种学习基延续方法），在14次从无约束到约束形式的转换中保持准确率不变，证明了写入几何是因果状态变量。在GSM8K、MathQA和AQuA数据集上，每模块top-k延续在k∈{2,4}达到最优，学习的top-16和top-32子集优于随机子集，且存在单方向消融揭示出的稀疏高影响组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低秩适配（LoRA）固定了更新矩阵的秩，但没有人去询问训练好的写入矩阵中哪些部分真正承载了行为。作者希望直接研究这一问题，揭示LoRA写入中行为信息的真实空间分布，从而理解模型微调中"几何位置"本身的重要性。

Method: 作者提出Learned-Basis LoRA学习基延续配方法：先以无约束适配器热身训练，将其训练好的写入列转换为模块级正交基并冻结该基，然后在受约束的参数化空间中继续训练。通过"同状态延续"实验证明不同写入子空间导致同一检查点不同发展；通过"无重训练投影"测试区分学习写入空间与随机/冻结激活PCA控制空间的信号保留情况；并通过每模块top-k延续和全局top-k排序测试来验证稀疏集中模式。

Result: 跨14次精确转换（无约束到约束形式），保持准确率不变，重建写入矩阵相对Frobenius误差≤0.25%。在GSM8K、MathQA、AQuA三个数据集上，12个seed级别测试全部在k∈{2,4}达到最优。全局排名测试中，学习到的top-16和top-32子集优于随机子集（尤其GSM8K/Qwen与MathQA/Qwen）。单方向消融显示晚期的q_proj、o_proj和down_proj组件存在超强行为影响的稀疏集合。

Conclusion: 行为有效的LoRA写入是高度稀疏和结构化的，其集中度远超原始低秩参数化所暗示的水平。写入几何（write geometry）是一个因果状态变量：相同的检查点在不同于空间中会发展出不同的行为。该发现对低秩微调的理论理解和实践设计（如稀疏LoRA、子空间选择策略）都具有重要意义。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation fixes the rank of the update, but it does not identify which parts of a trained
  write actually carry behavior. We study that question directly and show that behaviorally effective
  LoRA writes are sparse, structured, and far more concentrated than the raw low-rank parameterization
  suggests.
  We use Learned-Basis LoRA, a learned-basis continuation recipe, to expose that structure. The recipe
  warms up an unconstrained adapter, converts its learned write columns into a module-wise orthonormal
  basis, freezes that basis, and continues training inside the constrained parameterization. Across 14
  exact switches from unconstrained to constrained form, held-out accuracy is unchanged at the
  conversion step and reconstructed write matrices differ by at most 0.25% relative Frobenius error.
  Same-state continuation then shows that the same trained checkpoint develops differently under
  different write subspaces, establishing write geometry as a causal state variable. A no-retraining
  projection test shows that useful write signal stays inside the learned write space and largely
  disappears from random or frozen-activation PCA controls.
  The concentration pattern is strong at both local and global scales. Across GSM8K, MathQA, and AQuA,
  per-module top-k continuation reaches its optimum at k in {2, 4} in all twelve seed-level cases we
  test. A stricter global ranking test shows that learned top-16 and top-32 subsets outperform matched
  random subsets, especially on GSM8K/Qwen and MathQA/Qwen. Single-direction ablations further reveal a
  sparse set of late q_proj, o_proj, and down_proj components with outsized behavioral impact.

</details>


### [228] [Polish ModernBERT: The Long and Short of Polish Language Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01379)
*Michał Perełkiewicz,Sławomir Dadas,Rafał Poświata,Małgorzata Grębowiec*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Polish ModernBERT，一组波兰语编码器模型家族（Base和Large两个规模，各含512-token和8K上下文变体），基于ModernBERT预训练方案适配。在30个任务上取得波兰语编码器最佳综合性能，8K变体在长上下文任务上显著超越RoBERTa基线，且参数更少、效率更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 波兰语的编码器模型仍主要依赖BERT/RoBERTa架构，而Encoder-only Transformer在判别性和表征学习任务中依然有效。作者希望通过引入ModernBERT架构来填补波兰语模型在这一方面的空白，提升长上下文处理能力和效率。

Method: 构建波兰ModernBERT家族，包含四个模型（Base/Large × 512-token/8K上下文），通过分阶段选择实验适配ModernBERT预训练流程。同时发布了覆盖法律主题分类、意识形态决策方向预测、文学情节摘要的事实一致性评估以及人权侵犯评估的长上下文基准。使用低于300M参数的波兰语检索基准进行额外评估。

Result: 在30个任务上，Polish ModernBERT取得综合最佳表现（Base-8K达83.99，Large-8K达85.11）。长上下文任务上，8K变体将匹配的波兰RoBERTa-8K基线从67.47提升至77.15（Base）和从75.88提升至78.49（Large）。Base-8K以22%更少参数（149M vs 190M）实现该提升。效率测试显示峰值内存和延迟均低于RoBERTa基线。Base-8K在低于300M参数的波兰检索基准中取得最佳成绩。

Conclusion: Polish ModernBERT在波兰语编码器任务中实现了全面领先，兼具更长上下文支持、更低参数量和更高推理效率，证明ModernBERT架构可有效迁移至非英语语言场景。

Abstract: Encoder-only Transformers remain effective for discriminative and representation-learning tasks, yet Polish encoders still largely rely on BERT/RoBERTa-style architectures. We introduce \textbf{Polish ModernBERT}, a family of four Polish encoders available at Base and Large scales, each with 512-token and 8K context variants. We adapt the ModernBERT pretraining recipe through staged selection experiments and release a long-context benchmark covering legal topic classification, ideological decision-direction prediction, factual-consistency assessment over literary plot summaries, and human-rights violation assessment. Across 30 tasks, Polish ModernBERT achieves the best overall performance among the evaluated Polish encoders, reaching 83.99 and 85.11 for the Base-8K and Large-8K models, respectively. On long-context tasks, the 8K variants improve over matched Polish RoBERTa-8K baselines from 67.47 to 77.15 and from 75.88 to 78.49 at the Base and Large scales, respectively. The Base-8K model achieves this gain with 22\% fewer parameters (149M vs.\ 190M). Efficiency measurements in representative inference setups show lower peak memory usage and latency than matched Polish RoBERTa baselines in both 512-token and 8K settings. Polish ModernBERT-8K-Base additionally achieves the best result on a Polish retrieval benchmark among the evaluated encoders below 300M parameters.

</details>


### [229] [InSight: A Benchmark for Agentic Claim Verification in Interactive Visualizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01383)
*Maeve Hutchinson,Syed Mahbubul Huq,Mohammad Albinhassan,Radu Jianu,Aidan Slingsby,Pranava Madhyastha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出InSight，一个用于交互式可视化环境中智能体声明验证（agentic claim verification）的基准测试。该数据集包含21,349条源自人类分析叙事文本的声明，并锚定于完全交互式的Web环境。智能体需在这些环境中导航，判断自然语言声明的支持、反驳或无法验证状态。与传统评估不同，InSight将交互轨迹视为推理的内在代理，从而对模型如何寻找和综合视觉证据进行严格审计。实验表明，最先进模型在交互式验证任务上仍面临显著挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型在静态图像理解上表现优异，但现代数据分析本质上是动态的，需要主动与交互式环境进行交互。现有基准主要局限于静态图像和单轮问答，未能捕捉该领域认知层面的需求——在这些场景中，证据常常被遮挡、分布于多个关联视图中，或通过用户操作条件性揭示。

Method: 作者构建InSight数据集，包含21,349条来自人类编写分析叙事文本的声明，并将其锚定在完全交互式的Web可视化环境中。评估框架要求智能体在环境中主动导航以判定声明是否被支持、反驳或无法验证。与传统评估不同，InSight将交互轨迹视为内在推理代理，从而可以严格审查模型如何搜索和综合视觉证据。

Result: 对最先进模型的评估揭示了交互式验证仍然是一个非平凡挑战，表明当前VLM在动态、需要主动交互的数据分析场景中能力不足。

Conclusion: InSight填补了交互式可视化环境中智能体声明验证基准的空白，其发布（开源链接）将有助于推动该方向的研究，促使模型在真实数据分析场景中具备更强的主动推理与证据获取能力。

Abstract: Vision Language Models have demonstrated remarkable proficiency in interpreting static visual artifacts, but modern data analysis is inherently dynamic, requiring the active interrogation of interactive environments. Existing benchmarks are predominantly constrained to static imagery and one-shot question answering and fail to capture the epistemic demands of this domain, where evidence is frequently occluded, distributed across linked views, or conditionally revealed through user agency. In this paper, we introduce InSight, a benchmark for agentic claim verification over interactive visualizations. The dataset consists of 21,349 claims derived from human-authored analytical narratives and grounded in fully interactive web-based environments. Agents must navigate these environments to determine whether a natural language claim is supported, refuted or not verifiable given the available evidence. Unlike traditional evaluations, InSight treats interaction traces as intrinsic proxies for reasoning, enabling a rigorous audit of how models seek and synthesize visual evidence. We evaluate state-of-the-art models, revealing that interactive verification remains a non-trivial challenge. We release InSight at https://github.com/maevehutch/insight.

</details>


### [230] [When Tokenization is Secretly Output Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01386)
*Tanja Baeumel,Josef van Genabith,Simon Ostermann*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出应将分词（tokenization）视为输出监督（output supervision）而非仅仅是输入预处理决策。通过数值推理的受控实验，证明在自回归模型中，输出分词的粒度决定了模型单次前向传播需解决的任务难度与监督信号，从而影响任务性能、训练动态与模型内部表征，且这些影响与输入分词基本无关。对120篇*CL数值推理论文的调查显示仅约10%报告了数值分词方式，69%跨分词（即跨监督）制度比较却未报告，指出模型间比较可能部分反映任务定义差异而非能力差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究记录了大量分词影响模型性能的现象，但缺乏对"为什么分词如此重要"的机理层面解释。作者认为传统将分词视作输入预处理决策的框架不完整，需要从输出监督角度重新理解分词的深层作用机制。

Method: 作者采用以下研究方法：（1）提出"分词即输出监督"的理论框架，指出自回归模型中输出分词粒度决定单次前向传播需解析的目标；（2）设计受控实验，实现了输入与输出分词的新型解耦（decoupling）操作，以隔离两种分词各自的影响；（3）在数值推理任务上比较不同分词策略下的任务性能、训练动态及模型内部表征；（4）对120篇近期*CL论文进行系统的文献调查，统计数值分词报告率及跨分词比较的比例。

Result: 实验结果显示：任务性能、训练动态与模型内部表征的差异主要由输出分词引起，而对输入分词基本不敏感，与"输出监督"预测一致。文献调查显示，仅约10%的*CL论文报告了所评估模型的数值分词方式，69%的论文在跨分词（即跨监督）制度下进行比较而未予以说明。

Conclusion: 分词应被重新概念化为输出监督决策，而非仅输入预处理。由于不同分词策略使模型实际训练的任务不同，跨分词制度的模型比较可能混淆了"任务定义差异"与"能力差异"。该框架为先前观察到的分词影响性能的现象提供了统一的机理解释，并呼吁研究社区在报告与比较中显式披露分词策略。

Abstract: Tokenization in language models is treated by default as an input preprocessing decision. We argue that this framing is incomplete: in autoregressive models, tokenizer granularity determines what the model must resolve in a single forward pass, and therefore the supervision signal it receives. This affects both the difficulty of the learning problem and the representations that emerge inside the model. We test this in a controlled experiment on numeric reasoning with a novel decoupling of input and output tokenization. As the output supervision view predicts, differences in task performance, training dynamics, and model internals are induced by output tokenization and largely invariant to input tokenization. This may matter in practice, because models with different tokenization strategies differ not only in input representation but in the task they were trained on. Comparisons between models may thus partly reflect task definition rather than ability. A survey of 120 recent *CL papers on numeric reasoning confirms that this is rarely acknowledged: only about 10% report the numeric tokenization of the models they evaluate, while 69% compare across tokenization, and thus supervision, regimes without reporting it. While prior work documents that tokenization consistently affects model performance, there is no principled account of why. We argue that framing tokenization as output supervision provides that account.

</details>


### [231] [From Rollouts to Recipes: Self-Contained Post-Training for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01422)
*Yifei Li,Lingling Zhang,Muye Huang,Zihan Ma,Jiashuai Liu,Jun Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Self-Routing，一种基于行为状态的LLM后训练框架，根据样本的rollout正确性和置信度动态路由到GRPO、自蒸馏、正则化或跳过四种优化策略，无需外部教师或额外标注，在数学推理任务上一致优于均匀GRPO、均匀OPSD、固定混合及简单路由基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LLM后训练对所有样本采用单一训练配方，忽略了模型自身rollout所反映的不同样本级学习状态（如正确与否、置信度高低），导致训练低效且无法针对性地优化不同状态的样本。

Method: Self-Routing框架基于rollout正确性和置信度判断每个样本的行为状态，根据状态将样本路由至四种策略之一：GRPO（强化学习策略优化）、on-policy自蒸馏（向正确高置信样本学习）、正则化（防止过拟合或保持稳定性）、或直接跳过（对低信号或已稳定样本减少更新）。整个过程无需外部教师、额外标注或额外采样。

Result: 在Qwen3和Qwen3.5 backbone上进行的数学推理任务实验中，Self-Routing相较于均匀GRPO、均匀OPSD、固定混合策略及更简单的路由基线持续取得改进。进一步分析表明路由分布随训练过程动态变化，并有效减少了低信号或已稳定样本上的不必要更新。

Conclusion: Self-Routing证明了按样本行为状态进行自适应路由可以提升后训练效率与效果，是一种无需外部辅助资源的、可替代均匀训练配方的有效后训练范式。

Abstract: Post-training large language models usually applies a single training recipe to all samples, even though the model's own rollouts reveal different sample-level learning states. We propose Self-Routing, a behavior-conditioned post-training framework that uses rollout correctness and confidence to decide how each sample should be optimized. Depending on its behavior state, a sample is routed to GRPO, on-policy self-distillation, regularization, or skipping, allowing training to adapt without external teachers, extra annotations, or additional sampling. Experiments on mathematical reasoning across Qwen3 and Qwen3.5 backbones show that Self-Routing consistently improves over uniform GRPO, uniform OPSD, fixed mixtures, and simpler routing baselines. Further analyses show that the routing distribution changes over training and reduces unnecessary updates on low-signal or already stable samples.

</details>


### [232] [GlossoGen: Emergent Language in Complex Multi-Agent LLM Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01491)
*Elias Stengel-Eskin,Newton Sander,Carlos Bonetti,Sasha Boguraev,James Bowler,Hale Sirin,Simon Kirby*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了GlossoGen平台，用于研究多LLM智能体场景中的语言演化。在SaveVeyru场景中发现LLM智能体之间确实发生语言演化，产生的语言具有组合性和形态多产性，但偏离英语先验并变得人类不可理解。语言演化依赖于效率压力、模型强度和后验阶段，且强模型是新颖语言涌现的必要条件，弱模型可以在语言涌现后学习已有语言，表明当前LLM具备此前仅人类具有的累积文化演化潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体之间以日益增长的速度交互，引发了对多智能体环境中语言演化的关键问题，这涉及安全性和可监控性，以及对LLM语言学理论的启示。此前尚未有系统研究LLM智能体在复杂场景下的语言演化现象。

Method: 开发GlossoGen平台，构建SaveVeyru场景（要求持有部分信息的智能体在压力下通信）。通过实验观察LLM智能体间的语言演化过程，并系统性地操控条件（如效率压力、模型强度、后验阶段存在与否）来识别语言演化的关键因素，同时考察新智能体的语言习得与传播机制。

Result: LLM智能体间确实发生语言演化，产生的语言是组合性的且形态多产，但偏离英语先验并变得人类不可理解。发现效率压力、支撑模型的强度和"后验"阶段是语言演化的必要条件。新智能体可以仅从使用中学会新语言并主动参与学习，弱模型虽不能创造新语言但可以学习已涌现的语言。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，当前LLM具备此前仅人类才被证实的累积文化演化潜力。混合智能体群体能够发展出超越其最低共同能力水准的能力。

Abstract: The growing rate at which LLM agents interact with one another raises key questions about language evolution in multi-LLM-agent settings, with implications for safety and monitorability as well as for linguistic accounts of LLMs. To address these questions, we introduce GlossoGen, a novel platform for studying multi-agent language evolution in complex scenarios. Within GlossoGen, we build the SaveVeyru scenario, which requires agents with partial information to communicate under pressure. We find that language evolution does occur between LLM agents, that the resulting languages are compositional and morphologically productive, and that they deviate from the LLMs' English prior in ways that render them incomprehensible to humans. Moreover, we identify several qualities essential to this evolution: pressure towards efficiency; the strength of the models backing the agents; and access to a "postmortem" stage in which agents can agree on linguistic conventions. Importantly, we observe that different conditions govern the transmission of language to new agents. Specifically, we find that agents learn new languages from usage alone, take an active role in this learning, and that while stronger models are required for novel language emergence, weaker models can learn an existing language once it has emerged. Taken together, our results indicate that current LLMs have the potential for cumulative cultural evolution -- previously attested only in humans -- with mixed populations of agents developing capacities that go beyond their lowest common denominator.

</details>


### [233] [Knowledge Distillation During Mid-Training Favors Reasoning over Factual Recall](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01532)
*Jacqueline He,Howard Yen,Shuyue Stella Li,Margaret Li,Hanqing Zeng,Yinglong Xia,Benyu Zhang,Zhuokai Zhao,Qiang Zhang,Pang Wei Koh,Luke Zettlemoyer,Wen-tau Yih*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Switch Distillation方法，用于解决logit知识蒸馏在模型中期训练阶段对事实记忆获取的负面影响。通过分析发现，标准的前向KL蒸馏在预训练阶段能同时提升推理和事实记忆，但在中期训练阶段反而拖慢事实记忆获取，原因是教师对不同数据域的置信度不对称。Switch Distillation通过教师预测熵作为路由信号，仅在教师置信度高的token上进行蒸馏，其余token回退到交叉熵损失，在推理、知识与常识性能上显著提升，同时几乎完整保留事实记忆能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准logit知识蒸馏（前向KL）在小语言模型训练中被广泛使用，但其收益在不同训练阶段是否一致并不清楚。实验发现其行为因训练阶段而异——预训练阶段有效，但中期训练阶段会损害事实记忆获取。作者希望找出根源并提出解决方案，使蒸馏在中后期训练中也能全面提升模型能力。

Method: 首先通过受控实验确认前向KL蒸馏在预训练与中期训练之间的阶段依赖性差异，并归因于（1）教师对程序性数据比知识密集型数据更自信，以及（2）学生在训练早期更早获得低熵事实知识这一不对称性。随后提出Switch Distillation：使用教师预测熵作为轻量级路由信号，仅在教师置信度高的token上执行蒸馏，否则回退到标准交叉熵损失。

Result: Switch Distillation在不同教师规模下均优于现有蒸馏目标。相对于标准NTP，它实现1.61-1.71倍的推理性能、1.13-1.19倍的知识与常识性能，并保留96.7-96.8%的事实记忆。在后期训练之后，Switch Distillation完全弥补事实记忆差距，同时维持推理（1.25-1.32倍）和知识/常识（1.13-1.20倍）的提升。

Conclusion: 蒸馏方法的有效性具有训练阶段依赖性，不能一刀切地使用。Switch Distillation通过基于教师置信度的token级路由，在中期训练中兼顾了推理能力提升与事实记忆保留，且收益在后期训练后依然持续，为阶段自适应的蒸馏策略提供了新的设计思路。

Abstract: Logit-based knowledge distillation (KD) is used to train smaller language models (LMs) via supervision from stronger teachers, but whether its benefits are consistent across training stages remains unclear. Through controlled experiments, we find that forward Kullback-Leibler (KL) distillation--the standard KD formulation--with post-trained teachers behaves fundamentally differently during mid-training, an intermediate phase of self-supervised learning on curated corpora. Surprisingly, while forward KD simultaneously improves reasoning and factual recall during pre-training relative to standard next-token prediction (NTP), it instead slows factual recall acquisition during mid-training despite continued reasoning gains. We trace this stage dependence to an asymmetry in teacher confidence across data domains and the student's evolving knowledge state: teachers are more confident on procedural than knowledge-intensive data, while students acquire low-entropy factual knowledge earlier in training. To mitigate this imbalance, we propose Switch Distillation, a simple mid-training objective that distills on tokens where the teacher is confident, using teacher predictive entropy as a lightweight routing signal, and otherwise falls back to cross-entropy. Switch Distillation consistently outperforms existing distillation objectives across teacher sizes. Relative to standard NTP, it achieves 1.61-1.71x the reasoning performance and 1.13-1.19x the knowledge and commonsense performance while preserving 96.7-96.8% of factual recall. Crucially, these benefits persist after post-training: Switch Distillation closes the factual recall gap while maintaining 1.25-1.32x and 1.13-1.20x gains in reasoning and knowledge and commonsense, respectively.

</details>


### [234] [SDARE-Bench: Evaluating Large Language Models on Conversational Stigma Detection and Response in Dyadic and Group Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01548)
*Stephanie Fong,Yiwen Jiang,Zimu Wang,Hongxi Yang,Yaling Shen,Hiu Weh Naomi Chow,Heung Ying Lai,Xiangyu Zhao,Qingyang Xu,Zhongxing Xu,Jiahe Liu,Guilherme C. Oliveira,Vincent Lee,Zongyuan Ge,Dominic Dwyer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出首个基于场景的基准测试SDARE-Bench，用于评估大语言模型（LLM）在污名检测与开放域回复生成中的表现。研究发现8个LLM在污名识别方面表现差，尤其群体对话场景更差；在开放回复生成中，群体场景比双人场景产生更多污名表达，且群体压力条件下污名表达率高达97.5%，揭示污名应对是LLM在复杂社会对话情境中的系统性安全漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM越来越多地参与可能影响社会判断的建议寻求与决策，但针对污名（stigma）的评估基准仍然稀缺。现有通用领域评测通常依赖静态提示和固定格式任务，忽略了日常沟通中的对话语境和受众效应（audience effects）。这构成了评估LLM污名相关行为的重要空白。

Method: 作者构建SDARE-Bench，一种基于场景的基准，同时评估LLM的污名检测能力（stigma detection）和开放式回复生成能力（open-ended response generation）。数据集包含1,138条双人对话查询和1,388条群体对话。评测横跨8个LLM。回复质量采用在1,392条人类标注回复上训练的分类器进行评估，并将数据集扩展至构造的群体压力场景进行进一步测试。

Result: 实验结果显示：8个LLM在污名成分识别上普遍表现不佳，群体对话中表现尤差。在开放式回复生成中，群体场景下的污名表达显著高于双人场景，对污名的抵抗能力更弱，给出的建议更不切实际。在构造的群体压力设置下，污名表达率飙升至平均97.5%的惊人水平。

Conclusion: 研究识别出污名应对是LLM反复出现的安全漏洞，尤其是在社会复杂的对话语境（如群体对话）中更为突出。这表明需要针对污名设计专门的评估基准和防御机制，特别是在群体互动和受众压力等真实世界交流情境下。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used in advice seeking and decision making that may affect social judgements. Despite stigma's profound effects on people and communities, benchmarks remain scarce. Existing general-domain evaluations typically rely on static prompts and fixed-format tasks, overlooking conversational contexts and audience effects in everyday communication. To address these gaps, we introduce SDARE-Bench, the first scenario-based benchmark evaluating both stigma detection and open-ended response generation in LLMs, comprising 1,138 dyadic queries and 1,388 group dialogue. Empirical results across 8 LLMs consistently demonstrate poor identification of stigma components, especially in group dialogues. In open-ended response generation, stigma expression was substantially higher in group settings than in dyadic, with weaker resistance to stigma and more unrealistic advice. Responses were evaluated using a classifier trained on 1,392 human annotated responses. In constructed group pressure settings, stigma expression rates further increased to a striking average of 97.5%. Our findings identify stigma response as a recurring LLM safety vulnerability, especially in socially complex conversational contexts.

</details>


### [235] [A systematic Approach to constructing a Chance-and-Risk Matrix for Semiconductor Supply Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01563)
*Ema Salkić,Alexander Fichtl,Philipp Ulrich,Hans Ehm,Marta Bonik,Georg Groh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个端到端流水线，自动从半导体公司的公开企业披露中提取、结构化和排序风险情报。该流水线利用大语言模型抽取风险与机会，构建知识图谱，并通过三层排序机制（算法公式、LLM相关性调整、专家验证）去重和评分。应用于五家价值链公司后产出76,207条评分项，独立校验有效率达92.6%，风险与机会排名与专家判断的平均Spearman相关系数分别为0.55和0.72。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半导体供应链面临地缘政治紧张、地理集中和快速技术变革带来的日益升级的风险，但目前缺乏可扩展的系统来从公开企业披露中持续提取、结构化并按优先级排列风险情报。

Method: 构建端到端流水线：首先检索半导体公司的企业文档，使用大语言模型提取文档描述的风险与机会；然后将其组织成知识图谱，连接每一条目与其类别、来源和相关事件；接着进行重复合并，并通过三层排序机制（算法公式、LLM相关性调整、专家验证）进行评分排序。

Result: 将流水线应用于五家覆盖价值链不同环节的公司，共产出76,207条评分项；独立检查确认92.6%的条目有效。自动化排名与专家判断的平均Spearman相关系数在风险维度为0.55，在机会维度为0.72。生成的风险矩阵识别出贸易限制是跨公司的主导性风险。

Conclusion: 该流水线展示了大规模自动化提取和排序供应链风险情报的可行性，其产出在有效性和与专家判断一致性方面均达到可接受水平，并成功识别出贸易限制作为半导体供应链中最突出的跨公司共性风险。

Abstract: Semiconductor supply chains face escalating risks from geopolitical tensions, geographic concentration, and rapid technological shifts, yet no scalable system continuously extracts, structures, and prioritizes risk intelligence from public corporate disclosures. We present an end-to-end pipeline that retrieves corporate documents for semiconductor companies and uses large language models (LLMs) to extract the risks and opportunities they describe. It organizes these into a knowledge graph linking each item to its category, sources, and related events, then merges duplicates and ranks them with a three-layer mechanism combining an algorithmic formula, an LLM relevance adjustment, and expert validation. Applied to five companies across the value chain, the pipeline produces 76,207 scored items, of which an independent check finds 92.6% valid. The automated rankings match expert judgment at an average Spearman correlation of 0.55 for risks and 0.72 for opportunities, and the resulting matrices identify trade restrictions as the dominant cross-company risk.

</details>


### [236] [From Confusion to Clarity: Confusion-Aware Retrieval and Knowledge Injection for Text Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01564)
*Manish Gupta,Chaitanya Giri,Jayasimha Talur*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一个无需微调的框架，通过识别LLM难以区分的标签对、扩展候选集包含易混淆标签、并生成针对性规则来区分相似候选，在三个基准上将Macro F1提升至多10.0个百分点，且生成的规则可迁移至更小更便宜的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在处理含大量语义相似标签的分类任务时表现不佳，因为这些标签间的区别是领域特定的，预训练阶段并未捕获。现有的top-K候选检索虽减少了候选数量，但无法帮助模型区分相似标签——当两个相似标签同时出现在候选中时，模型缺少正确选择的信号。

Method: 提出一个三步骤框架：(1)识别模型难以区分的标签对；(2)扩展候选集以包含易混淆标签；(3)生成针对性规则来区分相似候选。整个过程无需微调。

Result: 在WOS、Flipkart、LEDGAR三个基准上，相比检索基线Macro F1提升至多10.0个百分点；通过跨模型迁移，较小的模型（2B-20B）获得至多11.5个百分点的提升。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了大标签空间下相似标签难以区分的问题，且生成的规则具有可迁移性，能让更小、更经济的模型也从中受益。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) struggle to classify text into taxonomies with many semantically similar labels, as the distinctions are domain-specific and not captured by pre-training. To handle large label spaces, a common approach retrieves top-$K$ candidate labels by embedding similarity and prompt the LLM to choose among them. However, top-$K$ retrieval reduces the number of candidates but does not help the model tell similar ones apart. When two similar labels both appear as candidates, the model lacks the signal to choose correctly between them. We propose a framework that (1) identifies which label pairs the model struggles to distinguish, (2) expands the candidate set to include confusable labels, and (3) generates targeted rules to differentiate between similar candidates. The framework requires no fine-tuning, and the generated rules transfer to smaller, cheaper models. On three benchmarks (WOS, Flipkart, LEDGAR), our approach improves Macro F1 by up to 10.0pp over retrieval baselines, with smaller models (2B--20B) gaining up to 11.5pp via cross-model transfer.

</details>


### [237] [From Production Traffic to Post-Training: Building a Self-Hosted LLM That Covers the Corporate Request Mix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01572)
*Olga Tsymboi,Dmitrii Stoianov,Ramil Latypov,Danil Taranets,Daniil Dryabin,Mikhail Gashkov,Viktor Zelenkovskiy,Aleksandr Fida,Gleb Alektorov,Nikita Gulyakov,Arthur Babkin,Aleksandr Medvedev,Pavel Gein,Anatolii Potapov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种企业内部模型整合策略：将200+内部应用的流量整合到单一模型上，通过生产错误分析识别三个质量轴（指令遵循、函数调用、内部任务分布）的差距，并采用"每轴独立训练GRPO专家+两阶段SLERP合并"的方法，在不联合优化所有目标（避免跨域奖励干扰）的情况下，用一个推理模式模型在内部竞技场上超越约7倍参数量的基线模型，以极低的服务成本吸收50%平台流量（每月1.16亿请求）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驻留约束迫使企业自托管LLM，而持续采用新模型却不停用旧模型会导致服务集群不断扩大，碎片化有限的GPU资源池。作者的目标是在不扩大服务规模的前提下，通过模型整合来降低成本并提高资源利用率。

Method: 1) 从200+内部应用中合并流量到单一模型；2) 通过生产错误分析沿三个轴（指令遵循、函数调用、内部任务分布）识别质量差距；3) 质量通过按生产流量分层且由确定性验证器或校准LLM评判员评分的离线基准追踪；4) 为避免跨域奖励干扰，不用联合优化，而是每个轴训练独立的GRPO专家，再通过两阶段SLERP合并；5) 每个专家的奖励暴露了不同失败模式（语义坍缩、过度调用、冗长疾攻），各自需要特定修复。
